11. What these results do NOT establish

  • No final CCEF-Net classification accuracy is reported.
  • No learned multimodal fusion result is reported.
  • Nonempty OCR count is not OCR accuracy.
  • Cultural mention counts are not extraction accuracy.
  • Retrieval similarity is not truth confidence.
  • Claim grounding is not factual verification.
  • No historical test suite was rerun by this dashboard.
  • A missing report does not prove the corresponding work was not done.
